Environmental campaigners warn that a &quot;tidal wave&quot; of permanent chemical production is being launched to satisfy rising demand from the AI industry, specifically for data centers. A survey of the ten largest PFAS manufacturers found that most companies have concrete plans to increase production despite existing health concerns. PFAS, often referred to as &quot;forever chemicals,&quot; are persistent environmental pollutants that have been linked to cancer and other serious health issues.

rss · 观察家报\(The Observer\) · Sep 14, 06:00

**「PFAS, ChemSec, and AI Data Center Demand」** PFAS, or per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances, are known as &\#x27;forever chemicals&\#x27; because they do not break down in the environment and have been linked to adverse health effects. ChemSec is an international nonprofit organization that campaigns for safer chemical alternatives and has warned that the AI industry&\#x27;s need for specific semiconductor and data center cooling technologies is driving a surge in PFAS production. This new wave of manufacturing poses a significant challenge to the expanding regulatory frameworks in Europe, the UK, and the US that aim to restrict PFAS in consumer and industrial products.

**「Environmental Consequence」** The projected increase in PFAS manufacturing will intensify environmental contamination risks, potentially exacerbating health hazards associated with the persistent compounds in regions hosting AI infrastructure.

<a id="item-tech-news-80"></a>
### [China Issues Policy to Curb Payment Delays to SMEs](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181081.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

On September 14, 2026, the State Council of China issued a notice containing four categories of measures to address widespread payment delays affecting small and medium-sized enterprises \(SMEs\). The policy targets large companies that exploit their market advantage to extend payment cycles, which the government identified as a key vulnerability in supply chain resilience. Key measures require leading and state-owned enterprises to prioritize direct cash payments over electronic payment instruments, specifically committing to settle accounts within 60 days of delivery. The Chinese Ministry of Finance and the People&\#x27;s Bank of China are simultaneously regulating electronic voucher platforms, restricting their terms to prevent large corporations from deferring obligations and shifting financial burden onto smaller suppliers.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Context」** In China&\#x27;s supply chains, large buyers often use commercial bills of exchange and electronic payment vouchers to delay cash disbursement. These mechanisms function as deferred payment tools that allow core companies to maintain cash flow while forcing upstream SMEs to discount the instruments or seek high-cost financing to cover their own expenses before payment is received.

**「Consequences」** SMEs operating as suppliers to large commercial and state-owned enterprises will face stricter contractual payment deadlines and improved access to direct cash settlements. Simultaneously, large enterprises adopting these practices will receive targeted access to government-sponsored loan and bond financing programs to offset their short-term cash flow pressures.

**Tags**: `#Government Policy`, `#Supply Chain`, `#Finance`, `#Non-Technical`

<a id="item-tech-news-92"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily interviews French scholar on Tokyo War Crimes Tribunal documentary](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181046.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

People&\#x27;s Daily published an interview with Jean-Yves Le Naour, the French historian who scripted a France 2 documentary titled &quot;Tokyo Trials: The Forgotten Nuremberg,&quot; which aired in August 2025. The documentary focuses on the experiences of French judge Henri Bernard at the 1945-1948 Tokyo War Crimes Tribunal, using primary sources and original footage to explore the intersection of international law, postwar order, and political forces. Le Naour argues that the Tokyo Trial is less known in Europe than the Nuremberg Trial due to geographic distance, the two-and-a-half-year duration of the proceedings, and the onset of the Cold War, which shifted public attention. The article highlights the documentary&\#x27;s significant viewership of approximately 1.4 million viewers, indicating a strong French public interest in this overlooked historical period, and discusses the ongoing relevance of the tribunal&\#x27;s findings in addressing current Japanese historical revisionism and remilitarization.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** The Tokyo War Crimes Tribunal, also known as the International Military Tribunal for the Far East, opened in May 1946 and concluded in 1948, prosecuting 25 Japanese Class-A war criminals, of whom seven were sentenced to death. Along with the Nuremberg Tribunal, it established the precedent of individual criminal responsibility under international law for crimes against peace and crimes against humanity.

**「Impact」** For the French and broader European public, the documentary serves as a pivotal educational resource that challenges historical amnesia regarding the Asia-Pacific theater, prompting a reassessment of the Tokyo Tribunal&\#x27;s role in establishing international justice and countering contemporary revisionist narratives in Japan.

**Tags**: `#history`, `#politics`, `#geopolitics`, `#international\_relations`

---

<a id="item-tech-news-93"></a>
### [Zhongjian Island&\#x27;s Decades-Long Effort to Cultivate Greenery on a Barren Outpost](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181050.html) ⭐️ 0.0/10

People&\#x27;s Daily published a feature on the Chinese naval garrison at Zhongjian Island in the South China Sea&\#x27;s Xisha Islands, documenting decades of effort to establish vegetation on the barren 1.5-square-kilometer coral sand outpost. Soldiers began carrying soil from their home provinces to plant trees after initial attempts failed in the late 1970s, culminating in 1979 with the first surviving silver-haired tree. By 1982, nearly 300 coconut trees and pine trees had taken root, earning the unit the title &\#x27;Patriotic Island Sentinels&\#x27; from the Central Military Commission. The island&\#x27;s first coconut was finally harvested in 2002, 23 years after the trees were planted, and is now displayed in the garrison&\#x27;s honor room.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** Zhongjian Island, located at the southernmost point of the Xisha \(Paracel\) Islands chain, is a small coral and shell formation of less than 1.5 square kilometers. Its arid, salt-laden coral sand soil and frequent typhoons have historically made it nearly impossible to sustain plants or trees, which is why early garrisons could only see the colors of blue water and white sand upon arrival in 1975.

**「Impact」** The sustained cultivation efforts have transformed the barren island into a functioning garrison base with over 6,000 pine trees and hundreds of coconut trees, providing environmental stabilization and a lasting physical symbol of China&\#x27;s sovereign presence in the South China Sea.

**Tags**: `#Off-Topic`, `#Patriotic Story`, `#Military History`, `#No Tech Relevance`

### [Gonorrhoea and syphilis rates rise by over 50% in Australia](https://www.theguardian.com/australia-news/2026/sep/15/gonorrhoea-and-syphilis-rise-by-more-than-50-in-australia) ⭐️ 0.0/10

Newly collated data from the University of New South Wales’ Kirby Institute reveals that reported rates of gonorrhoea and infectious syphilis have increased by more than 50% across Australia over the past decade. In 2025, authorities recorded 42,393 gonorrhoea notifications and 5,986 infectious syphilis notifications, representing 10-year increases of 57% and 56%, respectively. Experts have described the upward trend as particularly concerning among women. The data also indicates that Indigenous Australians are experiencing disproportionately high rates of infection.

rss · 卫报\(The Guardian\) · Sep 14, 15:00

**「Context」** Gonorrhoea and syphilis are sexually transmitted infections that require public health monitoring to track epidemic trends and guide intervention strategies. The Kirby Institute is a major Australian research organization focused on sexual and HIV health, responsible for analyzing national notification data to identify emerging risks.

**「Consequences」** Public health officials and clinicians face increased pressure to expand diagnostic services, implement targeted prevention programs, and address disparities affecting women and Indigenous Australians. The sustained rise in cases suggests that current mitigation strategies may be insufficient to curb the decade-long upward trend.

### [China to Host BRICS 2027 Summit Following New Delhi Meeting](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181067.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 12-13, 2026, Chinese President Xi Jinping attended the 18th BRICS Leaders&\#x27; Summit in New Delhi, India, where he announced that China will assume the BRICS chairmanship in 2026 and host the 19th summit. The summit focused on themes of inclusive global governance and strengthened multilateralism, with Xi proposing four priorities for BRICS to lead innovation, peace, civilizational exchange, and global governance reform. He also unveiled five specific cooperation initiatives, including an AI open-source program, a digital industry cloud platform, and an engineer training alliance, aimed at fostering technological and economic collaboration among BRICS nations and the Global South.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** The BRICS grouping, which marks its 20th anniversary of its original &\#x27;BRIC&\#x27; formation in 2006, is a major forum for coordinating economic and political cooperation among emerging and developing nations. The annual BRICS chairmanship rotates among member states, with the host country convening the leaders&\#x27; summit and shaping the strategic agenda for the year.

**「Impact」** The announcement places the 2027 BRICS summit and associated technological cooperation frameworks squarely on the global diplomatic and economic agenda, positioning the group as a central counterweight to Western-led institutions while solidifying China&\#x27;s role in setting the pace of AI and industrial partnerships among developing nations.

The plan aims to promote high-quality development in veterans&\#x27; work during the 15th Five-Year Period \(2026–2030\) by deepening reforms in management, policy, and service systems. Key tasks include improving veteran employment, enhancing social support, upgrading care facilities, and strengthening ideological leadership. It also outlines specific projects such as improving the efficiency of veterans&\#x27; service centers, renovating martyrs&\#x27; memorial sites, and expanding medical and health care systems for preferential treatment.

<a id="item-tech-news-181"></a>
### [Pinglu Canal Completes Trial Navigation Ahead of Opening](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181069.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The 134.2-kilometer Pinglu Canal, the first such waterway connecting a major river to the sea in China&\#x27;s history, successfully completed its trial navigation on September 14, 2026, with the vessel &\#x27;Beigang Yunhe 001&\#x27; sailing to the Qinzhou Port. The project, which began construction in August 2022 and involved over 20,000 workers, features three cascading hubs to manage a 65-meter water level drop and innovates with a water-saving pool system that achieves a 60% savings rate. It establishes a cost-effective maritime route for the Southwest region, projecting a 18% to 30% reduction in logistics costs and annual savings of over 5 billion yuan for local industries. The canal operates alongside ecological preservation measures, including a complex fish passageway and a &\#x27;near-zero carbon&\#x27; clean energy corridor at its hubs.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Context」** The construction of the Pinglu Canal fulfills a blueprint for connecting the Southwest region to the sea first outlined by Sun Yat-sen in his book &\#x27;An Outline for the Construction of the Nation.&\#x27; Its development is a central component of the &\#x27;New Western Land and Sea Corridor&\#x27; initiative, which aims to facilitate a multi-modal transport network linking China&\#x27;s inland provinces with the ASEAN region.

**「Economic Consequences」** Southwestern manufacturers in Yunnan, Guizhou, Sichuan, and Chongqing gain direct access to the ASEAN market with significantly reduced freight costs, transforming their logistical disadvantages into a competitive advantage.

### [People&\#x27;s Daily Marks Peking Union Medical College Hospital&\#x27;s 105th Anniversary](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181070.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 15, 2026, People&\#x27;s Daily published a feature commemorating the 105th anniversary of Peking Union Medical College Hospital, founded on September 16, 1921. The article details the hospital&\#x27;s expansion in rare disease care, including the establishment of 55 multidisciplinary consultation groups, 5,000+ consultations, and a dedicated rare-disease ward in 2024 that reduced average diagnosis time from four years to under four weeks. In 2025, the hospital launched the &\#x27;Xiehe-Taihu&\#x27; rare-disease large model, which processed over 9,000 consultations and assisted in diagnosing more than 1,000 cases in its first year. The piece also highlights collaborative R&amp;D, including the reorganization of a national key laboratory in 2023 and ongoing &\#x27;group-style&\#x27; aid to Tibet and a medical center in Macau, with over 400 long-term experts deployed nationally since 2015.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** Peking Union Medical College Hospital, commonly called Xiehe, is one of China&\#x27;s leading tertiary medical centers, established in 1921 with ties to the Union Medical College. It has historically shaped modern Chinese clinical training through disciplines such as internal medicine, gynecology, and pathology, and now plays a national role in rare-disease diagnosis, research infrastructure, and capacity building in regions and territories outside mainland China.

**「Impact」** For patients with rare or undiagnosed diseases, the hospital&\#x27;s expanded multidisciplinary rare-disease services and AI-assisted diagnostic model have materially shortened diagnostic timelines, moving from multi-year waits to weeks for a significant share of admitted cases. For regional and cross-border systems, long-term expert deployment and the Macau medical center extend high-complexity care access, though the full scale of sustained clinical outcomes at these sites remains to be evaluated.

<a id="item-tech-news-185"></a>
### [Chinese Foreign Policy Focuses on Stability and Progress at SCO and BRICS Summits](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181073.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A September 2026 People&\#x27;s Daily analysis examines President Xi Jinping&\#x27;s diplomatic visits to the Shanghai Cooperation Organization \(SCO\) summit in Bishkek and the 18th BRICS meeting in New Delhi, framing them as key examples of China&\#x27;s &\#x27;stability&\#x27; and &\#x27;progress&\#x27; in foreign policy. To ensure global order, the article highlights China&\#x27;s commitment to genuine multilateralism, the collective self-reliance of the Global South, and the expansion of cooperative networks through the &\#x27;SCO+&\#x27; and &\#x27;BRICS+&\#x27; initiatives. The article points to concrete progress in development and governance, noting that over the past five years the Global Development Initiative has established more than 2,000 cooperation projects, trained over 200,000 personnel, and benefited more than 120 countries. It also references the July 2026 establishment of the World Artificial Intelligence Cooperation Organization and the subsequent proposal of an open-source AI inclusivity initiative at the BRICS meeting, which aims to share opportunities and co-create prosperity.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Institutional Context」** The Shanghai Cooperation Organization \(SCO\) is a Eurasian intergovernmental organization established in 2001 that recently marked its 25th anniversary in 2026, with Kyrgyzstan assuming the rotating presidency after the 2025 Tianjin summit. The BRICS group, initiated in 2009, represents the world&\#x27;s leading emerging-market economies and is transitioning into its third &\#x27;Golden Decade&\#x27; of cooperation. The World Artificial Intelligence Cooperation Organization is a newly established international body, created in July 2026 under Chinese initiative, designed to facilitate global and Global South cooperation in artificial intelligence applications and open-source development.

**「Impact」** For multilateral institutions like the SCO and BRICS, this diplomatic push sets a clear agenda for expanding their roles in global development, AI governance, and regional security architecture. Members and partner nations are expected to face new pressures to align with these frameworks, particularly in integrating open-source AI technologies and adopting a &\#x27;new Middle East security architecture&\#x27; focused on rebuilding mutual trust.

President Xi Jinping attended the 18th BRICS Summit in New Delhi from September 12 to 13, 2026, at the invitation of Indian Prime Minister Narendra Modi. During the summit, President Xi announced that China will assume the BRICS presidency and host the 19th summit next year, marking the fourth time China holds this role. He outlined four strategic directions for the bloc, including promoting innovation, maintaining peace, fostering cultural exchange, and reforming global governance. Additionally, China proposed practical initiatives in AI open-source cooperation, digital industry platforms, and engineer training to strengthen ties among the Global South and advance inclusive global development.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

This 18th summit focused on &\#x27;Inclusive Global Governance and Strengthening Multilateralism&\#x27; as a response to the growing influence of the Global South in international affairs.

**「Impact」** The summit formally established China&\#x27;s upcoming presidency of the BRICS bloc, which will serve as the institutional framework for the group&\#x27;s next phase of multilateral economic and technological cooperation among developing nations.

<a id="item-tech-news-195"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily editorial urges literature to ground in public life via new festivals](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181098.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A People&\#x27;s Daily commentary argues that literature must remain rooted in the public and urban life to maintain its vitality and power, especially as digital technologies have brought art closer to mass audiences. The editorial references the recently concluded &\#x27;2026 China Literary Festival · Lu Xun Literary Week&\#x27; in Shanghai, which expanded the Lu Xun Literature Prize&\#x27;s recognition into a week-long event across bookstores, parks, and community spaces. It cites a report showing that domestic online literature users reached 550 million with over 44 million cumulative works, and more than 12,000 adapted short-video or micro-drama titles by late 2025. The piece emphasizes that the &\#x27;15th Five-Year Plan&\#x27; outlines a goal for people to enjoy a higher-quality cultural life, positioning literature not as a basic need but as an indispensable element for spiritual fulfillment and social cohesion. It concludes that contemporary writers and institutions should help literature evolve into a lifestyle, fostering resonance among ordinary people in line with the legacy of Lu Xun.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** Lu Xun is China’s most influential modern writer, known for his sharp social criticism and literary innovations; his final decade was spent in Shanghai producing major works such as \*Story-rewriting of Ancient Tales\* and \*Dawn Blossoms Plucked at Dusk\*. The Lu Xun Literature Prize is a major award for Chinese literary achievement, and its accompanying events are now designed to extend public engagement beyond traditional award ceremonies.

**「Impact」** For Chinese literary institutions, policymakers, and grassroots creators, the editorial’s stance signals a policy and cultural expectation that literary awards and programming should be restructured to integrate into everyday public spaces and digital platforms. This shift may prioritize community-based events, online adaptations, and inclusive outreach over purely ceremonial or elite-focused recognition, thereby influencing how literature funding, publication, and promotion are organized in the coming years.

<a id="item-tech-news-197"></a>
### [Xinjiang desert village builds rice tourism economy](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181111.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Rice farming in Daoxiang Village, Hotan County, Xinjiang, has transformed from small-scale subsistence plots into a 1,800-acre concentrated rice field after standard farmland construction reclaimed and leveled land for unified commercial cultivation. Yields doubled from roughly 300 to 600 kilograms per acre as the village developed a “gold silk rice” brand. In 2021, Beijing&\#x27;s Xinjiang support task force provided funding and positioned the village as a countryside-tourism complex with “rice field +” offerings. By 2025, the village drew about 35,000 tourists and lifted average per-capita net income from 9,800 to over 14,800 yuan, while rice land-lease rents brought the village collective more than 1.1 million yuan extra annually. The village added agritainment elements such as courtyard restaurants, a courtyard music venue, a library, and hot-air-balloon sightseeing to diversify revenue.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Context」** Daoxiang Village \(formerly Ayakedun, meaning “last sand mound”\) sits beside the Taklamakan Desert and had been encircled by dunes 5 to 6 meters high. The village had over a century of rice-cropping history, drawing water naturally from the nearby Karakash River and Yulongkash River, which made paddy farming viable on desert margins despite harsh winds and sand.

**「Impact」** Villagers shifted from low-output subsistence farming to leasing land at 600 yuan per acre \(about 400 yuan more than self-cultivation\) while opening homestead restaurants and tourism services, raising household income and village collective revenue. Exact figures are as reported by the source and were not independently verified.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-198"></a>
### [Guangdong&\#x27;s 百千万工程 Boosts Rural Growth in Northern Regions](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181112.html) ⭐️ ?/10

In Guangdong&\#x27;s northern Shaoguan region, the &\#x27;百千万工程&\#x27; \(Hundred Counties, Thousand Towns, Ten Thousand Villages High-Quality Development Project\) has transformed rural economic weaknesses into growth potential, as highlighted in a September 15, 2026 People&\#x27;s Daily report. In Renshuixiang&\#x27;s Xinbai Village, tea farmers earn over 100 yuan per day after converting 480+ mu to higher-value Jinsand tea, lifting the village&\#x27;s collective annual income from 90,000 to over 400,000 yuan. In nearby Renshuixiang, 38,000 bamboo farming households increased income by 15,000 yuan each in 2025, pushing the county&\#x27;s total bamboo industry output to 1.589 billion yuan and creating jobs for over 5,000 people. In Leicheng City, local golden nectarines sold via e-commerce boosted incomes by over 30,000 yuan for 7,000+ farming households. Since the project&\#x27;s late-2022 launch, 57 counties in the region have seen GDP growth exceed the provincial average for two consecutive years, and the urban-rural income ratio narrowed from 2.41:1 to 2.28:1.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** The &\#x27;百千万工程&\#x27; was launched in late 2022 to address long-standing imbalances in Guangdong&\#x27;s urban-rural and regional development. It builds on national policy experiences like the &\#x27;Thousand Villages&\#x27; initiative and aims to leverage the ecological strengths of Guangdong&\#x27;s eastern, western, and northern regions to enhance economic capacity. This initiative also echoes the historical context of the 1934 Long March through these northern areas, framing modern rural revitalization as a continuation of that pioneering spirit.

**「Impact」** For local residents and businesses in Guangdong&\#x27;s rural northern areas, the initiative has directly increased household incomes and local GDP by creating value-added jobs in specialty agriculture, bamboo processing, and e-commerce. It has also reduced the urban-rural income gap in the participating 57 counties, marking a measurable success in regional economic rebalancing.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-202"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily Editorial on Cadres and Talents for National Rejuvenation](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181116.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 15, 2026, People&\#x27;s Daily published a feature article by Liu Jingbei, a distinguished researcher at the Shanghai Research Center for the Philosophy, Ideology and Politics of Xi Jinping, outlining a systematic framework for building a high-quality cadre and talent corps capable of undertaking the mission of national rejuvenation. The article emphasizes strict adherence to the Party&\#x27;s organizational line, which prioritizes political standards above all others, and mandates the &\#x27;Five Major Systems&\#x27; \(cultivation, identification, selection, management, and incentive\) to ensure a complete, coordinated talent pipeline. It integrates severe supervision with positive incentives to foster a culture of responsibility and performance, while simultaneously advancing a national strategy that prioritizes talent innovation and the bold promotion of young officials to leadership roles to secure the future of the Party and state.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** In China&\#x27;s political system, the &\#x27;organizational line&\#x27; governs how the Communist Party of China manages its own human resources, viewing cadre selection and management as the fundamental guarantee of its continued rule. This framework, central to Xi Jinping&\#x27;s党建 thought, evolved from historical principles of &\#x27;merit and virtue&\#x27; to a modern standard that explicitly subordinates professional competence to political loyalty, ensuring that party officials remain aligned with central directives.

**「Impact」** For Chinese political cadres and organizational departments, this editorial establishes a definitive operational standard that prioritizes political loyalty in promotion and mandates systematic, high-pressure supervision alongside targeted incentives to accelerate the professionalization of state leadership.

<a id="item-tech-news-207"></a>
### [Peking Union Medical College Hospital Celebrates 105th Anniversary at US-China Medical Exchange](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181036.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The US-China Medical Exchange Conference and the 105th anniversary academic week of Peking Union Medical College Hospital opened in Beijing in September 2026. Hospital Director Zhang Shuyang and Roger Glass, President of the Rockefeller Foundation&\#x27;s China Medical Board, signed the third five-year cooperation memorandum on September 10, building on 2016 and 2021 agreements focused on graduate medical education. The event gathered hundreds of experts to discuss AI governance, medical talent training, and rare disease diagnostics and treatment, marking Peking Union&\#x27;s establishment by Rockefeller-funded Chinese Medical Foundation in 1921. PUMCH reported reducing average rare disease diagnosis time from four years to four weeks through 55 multidisciplinary consulting teams that have conducted over 5,000 cases.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Historical Context of PUMCH」** Peking Union Medical College Hospital \(PUMCH\) was established in 1921 with funding from the Rockefeller Foundation via the China Medical Board, making it a pivotal institution in the history of modern Chinese medicine. As a leading national medical center, PUMCH serves as the guiding center for national diagnosis and treatment of rare diseases and leads the national rare disease diagnosis and treatment collaboration network, facilitating ongoing international medical cooperation and talent training.

**「Impact」** The memorandum commits both institutions to five-year cooperation in graduate medical education, clinical postdoctoral programs, and scientific research, directly affecting medical talent training pipelines in Chinese hospitals. PUMCH&\#x27;s multidisciplinary consultation network will continue scaling rare disease diagnostics, expanding collaboration with US, UK, and international partners.

<a id="item-tech-news-208"></a>
### [China and Switzerland Upgrade Free Trade Agreement](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181037.html) ⭐️ ?/10

China and Switzerland concluded the upgrade negotiations for their free trade agreement, signing a memorandum of understanding that represents the first upgraded FTA China has reached with a top-20 global economy and an EU-adjacent state. The upgrade, which began in September 2024 and spanned five rounds of consultations, establishes that over 99% of goods from both countries will be imported at zero tariffs. The new framework extends high-level, two-way liberalization to services and investment, and establishes detailed cooperation rules in digital trade, artificial intelligence, sustainable development, supply chains, pharmaceuticals, mechanical industries, and watchmaking. The agreement incorporates advanced institutional rules covering competition policy, intellectual property, government procurement, and labor standards to provide a transparent and predictable operating environment for businesses in both nations.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background Context」** The original bilateral free trade agreement was signed in 2013, marking a pioneering move for Switzerland at a time when Western partners were generally cautious about establishing FTAs with China. This initial agreement significantly boosted bilateral commerce, elevating China to the position of Switzerland&\#x27;s third-largest trading partner.

**「Significance」** The upgraded agreement serves as a structural &quot;pressure release valve&quot; for Swiss firms facing sustained trade policy pressure from the United States, while demonstrating to broader European economies that mutual economic integration with China remains a viable strategic alternative to confrontation. The new institutional framework provides a stable, predictable regulatory environment for Chinese and Swiss enterprises that actively operate in or plan to enter the respective markets.

<a id="item-tech-news-209"></a>
### [Hebei Province Boosts Land Security for 747 Key Projects](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181038.html) ⭐️ ?/10

As of August 2026, the Hebei Department of Natural Resources approved 120,400 mu of construction land, ensuring 93.98% security for the province&\#x27;s 747 key projects. The province achieved this by activating inter-departmental coordination and simplifying approval workflows, including a linked review system for land, forest, and sea use. Specific policy measures were introduced to compress project implementation timelines, such as reducing correction deadlines to ten working days. This coordinated administrative effort supports major infrastructure developments, including the Qinshen Expressway and the Dongguang wind power project.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** In China&\#x27;s administrative framework, land is a strictly regulated element required for all economic and social development. The Hebei department is integrating fragmented approval processes for land, forest, grassland, and sea resources to reduce bureaucratic bottlenecks.

**「Impact」** Developers and infrastructure enterprises in Hebei benefit from a 4.34-day average approval turnaround, which significantly accelerates the pre-construction phases of major transportation and energy projects. This improved administrative efficiency reduces the time lag between project planning and physical construction.

### [People&\#x27;s Daily Explains Style-Building &\#x27;Three Words&\#x27; Framework](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181064.html) ⭐️ ?/10

People&\#x27;s Daily published an article on September 15, 2026, explaining that advancing the normalization and long-term effectiveness of party style-building is a core principle of Xi&\#x27;s party-building thought. The article outlines the &\#x27;three words&\#x27; \(three key actions\) framework: constant attention to daily work \(抓常\), focusing on details \(抓细\), and ensuring long-term sustainability \(抓长\). Citing data from July 2026, the Central Commission for Discipline Inspection noted that 27,000+ violations of the Central Eight-Point Regulations were handled nationwide, marking the 155th consecutive month of such reports. Additionally, a 2025 National Bureau of Statistics survey found that 95.5% of the public affirmed the effectiveness of the Central Eight-Point Regulations.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** The Central Eight-Point Regulations \(中央八项规定\) are a set of anti-corruption and work-style directives introduced by the Chinese Communist Party in 2012, aimed at curbing extravagance and formalism. It serves as the cornerstone of the party&\#x27;s ongoing &\#x27;style-building&\#x27; \(作风建设\) campaign.

**「Impact」** For Chinese government officials and local cadres, the article serves as a formal directive reinforcing that adherence to anti-corruption standards and anti-formalism measures remains an ongoing, high-priority mandate rather than a one-time initiative.

<a id="item-tech-news-214"></a>
### [Cultural essay outlines the evolving role of Chinese folk songs](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181056.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A cultural essay published in People&\#x27;s Daily argues that Chinese folk songs, or &\#x27;minge&\#x27;, remain vital and are not inherently obsolete despite rapid social changes. The author highlights recent viral successes on social media, such as the Northwest &\#x27;Hua&\#x27;er&\#x27; song &\#x27;Galian Shou&\#x27; and the Jingpo ethnic &\#x27;Munao Song&\#x27;, which have garnered hundreds of millions of plays and demonstrated the renewed appeal of traditional music. The essay uses the twenty-year resurgence of Mongolian long songs on the Xilin Gol grassland as a case study, noting that younger generations are increasingly adopting and adapting the art form in both traditional settings and modern urban contexts. It cautions that successful innovation requires a deep understanding of a song&\#x27;s historical, geographic, and social roots, warning against superficially appropriating traditional musical symbols without grasping their underlying cultural context.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** Chinese folk songs are diverse traditional musical forms that are primarily transmitted through oral tradition and vary significantly by region and ethnic group. As China undergoes rapid urbanization and modernization, many of the everyday and ceremonial environments that historically supported these songs have diminished, leading to a loss of their traditional living context. However, contemporary media, educational institutions, and modern performance stages have created new avenues for their preservation and widespread dissemination.

**「Impact」** For contemporary musicians, cultural practitioners, and the public, the essay advocates that authentic creative transformation of folk music requires deep historical and cultural research to preserve the original traditions and prevent the loss of foundational knowledge. It highlights the importance of maintaining the practice of listening to traditional music within its specific geographic and lifestyle context to avoid reducing complex cultural heritage to superficial pop adaptations.

<a id="item-tech-news-216"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily Analyzes Why Viewers Urge &\#x27;Return Me Ji Jie&\#x27;](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/15/content_30181058.html) ⭐️ ?/10

People&\#x27;s Daily published an analysis on September 15, 2026, examining the social media phenomenon of \#ReturnMeJiJie, which emerged after the release of &\#x27;Major Case Squad: The Vanishing Badge,&\#x27; a reboot of the classic Chinese crime series &\#x27;Major Case Squad.&\#x27; The article argues that the backlash is not merely about performance or nostalgia but represents a complex shift in audience expectations. It frames the discourse as an aesthetic critique of perceived poor writing and acting, a nostalgic expression of collective cultural memory around the original character, and a call for creators to respect the &\#x27;canon&\#x27; of intellectual property in ways that align with audience sentiment, even when legal rights are held by the original production company.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 15, 00:26

**「Background」** &\#x27;Major Case Squad&\#x27; is a popular Chinese police procedural drama series from the late 1990s and early 2000s, known for its depiction of the &\#x27;Major Case Squad&\#x27; and its charismatic female lead, Ji Jie, portrayed by actress Xu Jinglei. In recent years, the franchise has seen revivals and derivative works, which frequently trigger debates about faithfulness to the original material among nostalgic viewers. The term &\#x27;canon&\#x27; \(正典权\) in IP development refers to the creative authority and boundaries of a story&\#x27;s universe, traditionally controlled by copyright holders, but increasingly contested by audiences who expect adaptations to honor the core spirit of the original character and setting.

**「Impact」** The sustained online discourse signals to IP developers and media producers in China that audience emotional investment and perceived fidelity to a character&\#x27;s established core traits are becoming critical success factors for revivals, often outweighing strict legal ownership of canon in determining public reception. This shift places a premium on narrative continuity and respectful adaptation to mitigate audience backlash against commercially-driven reboots.

The Trump family has a history of close interactions with Russian officials, which has often been subject to political scrutiny and debate regarding foreign influence.

The UK government has announced it will move to nationalise Speciality Steel UK \(SSUK\) to protect approximately 1,300 jobs across sites in Rotherham, Stocksbridge, Brinsworth, and Wednesbury. Business Secretary Jonathan Reynolds stated that officials would &quot;work towards&quot; acquiring the company after it failed to find a private buyer. SSUK, formerly part of Sanjeev Gupta’s corporate empire, entered administration in the previous year. This intervention places the steelworks into public ownership to safeguard employment in South Yorkshire and the West Midlands.

rss · 观察家报\(The Observer\) · Sep 14, 16:17

**「Background」** Speciality Steel UK is a manufacturer of high-grade steel used in specialized industrial applications and was previously part of the business empire controlled by industrialist Sanjeev Gupta. The company entered administration last year and has since failed to secure a new private owner, prompting government intervention.

**「Impact」** The 1,300 workers at the South Yorkshire and West Midlands sites are directly protected from potential redundancy through the government&\#x27;s acquisition of the struggling company. This move signals state support for the UK&\#x27;s domestic steel industry when commercial buyers are unavailable.

BioNTech&\#x27;s lung cancer drug gotistobart demonstrated a significant extension in median survival compared to standard-of-care chemotherapy. Patients treated with gotistobart lived for a median of 18.5 months, whereas those given the chemotherapy lived for a median of 10 months. This difference highlights the therapeutic advantage of gotistobart in prolonging life for lung cancer patients. No specific limitations, compatibility constraints, or additional performance data were provided in the source content.

rss · 日本时报\(The Japan Times\) · Sep 14, 11:57

**「Lung Cancer Treatment Context」** Standard-of-care chemotherapy is a conventional treatment regimen for lung cancer, typically associated with shorter median survival periods. Novel targeted therapies like gotistobart are developed to offer improved outcomes, with median survival being a key clinical endpoint for evaluating treatment efficacy. The comparison between targeted drug therapies and chemotherapy is a central focus in modern oncology research.

**「Impact on Lung Cancer Patients」** Lung cancer patients who receive gotistobart may experience a substantial improvement in median survival, extending their lives by approximately 8.5 months compared to those treated with standard chemotherapy. This specific outcome is directly supported by the reported median survival figures for gotistobart and chemotherapy.

**「Consequence」** Okinawa&\#x27;s local governance will shift toward economic development initiatives under the new administration, potentially altering the political balance regarding US base policies. The specific details of how economic priorities will override or coexist with base issues remain to be seen in policy implementation.

It also underscores the critical importance of protecting satellites that support military communications, surveillance, and navigation systems.

Many modern military operations rely on space-based assets like GPS and reconnaissance satellites for navigation and intelligence. The deployment of weapons in orbit shifts the strategic landscape toward active military control and protection of these essential assets.

Turkish police conducted raids on gay bars and the homes of LGBTQ activists on Sunday, arresting at least 60 individuals. Authorities stated the operations were aimed at boosting traditional family values, while officials and activists described the action as a targeted assault on the community. Turkish President Recep Tayyip Erdogan has frequently targeted the country&\#x27;s LGBTQ population, previously labeling them a “deviant movement” of “perverts” and blaming them for the nation’s falling birth rate. Yildiz Tar, Editor-in-Chief of KaosGL.org, emphasized that the government’s stance treats the very existence of LGBTQI people as obscene.

rss · France 24 · Sep 14, 21:02

**「Background」** The Turkish government has systematically restricted LGBTQ rights through policy and public rhetoric, with President Erdogan repeatedly framing sexual minorities as threats to national demographic and cultural values. This approach contrasts with international human rights standards, which generally protect individuals from discrimination based on sexual orientation and gender identity.

**「Impact」** The mass arrests and state-sponsored rhetoric significantly escalate the risk of persecution and legal exposure for LGBTQ individuals and organizations operating within Turkey.

The author suggests that assigning responsibility to creators is a necessary step to manage the risks associated with advanced AI systems.

The Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung published a profile of Maurizio Cattelan, described as one of the most important contemporary artists. The piece features a rare interview conducted on the Aeolian Islands ahead of a major exhibition in Berlin, where Cattelan discussed his personal history and artistic approach. He recounted leaving home at age seventeen with only two plastic bags, framing this early departure as a pivotal moment in his life. The article documents informal interactions during breakfast, a time at the harbor, and dinner with friends, providing a personal glimpse into the artist&\#x27;s daily life.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 14, 10:00

**「Context for Maurizio Cattelan&\#x27;s Berlin Exhibition」** Maurizio Cattelan, born in 1960 in Padua, is a leading Italian contemporary artist known for his concept art that often employs dark humor and irony to challenge the art market. The Neun Nationalgalerie in Berlin is presenting his first major solo exhibition in Germany, which opens on September 10, 2026, as part of the Nationalgalerie Prize. This rare media engagement reflects his reputation for avoiding public attention, making this personal account and upcoming display significant for understanding his current career phase.

### [Flight births will be registered on birth certificates in Russia](https://max.ru/tass_agency) ⭐️ ?/10

David Melik-Guseinov, deputy director of the Higher School of National Economy under the Russian Presidential Academy of National Economy and Public Administration, stated that the destination of the flight in which a child is born will be recorded on the child&\#x27;s birth certificate. This administrative clarification was provided following a recent incident where medical personnel successfully delivered a 30-year-old Russian woman on a plane traveling from Krasnoyarsk to Novy Urengoy. The rule ensures that the jurisdiction or administrative division of the arrival point is used for official documentation. This addresses a specific gap in civil registration practices for live births occurring within Russian airspace or on domestic flights.

telegram · tass\_agency · Sep 15, 00:43

**「Background」** Births during air travel are rare events that present specific logistical and legal challenges for civil registration systems. Typically, births are registered in the municipality where the event occurs, but a flight crosses multiple jurisdictions and international borders. Clarifying which location to use, such as the destination airport&\#x27;s district, simplifies the bureaucratic process for parents and authorities. This policy provides a standardized approach for documenting such unique circumstances within Russia.

**「Impact」** Parents who experience childbirth during air travel now have a clear administrative pathway for registering their children, using the destination&\#x27;s locality for the birth certificate, which reduces legal ambiguity in civil documentation.

<a id="item-tech-news-389"></a>
### [Astronomers identify 84 hypersoft X-ray sources in nearby galaxies](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41550-026-02959-7) ⭐️ ?/10

Astronomers using data from the Chandra X-ray Observatory identified 84 previously unrecognized objects across six nearby galaxies, including Andromeda and the Pinwheel Galaxy. These objects, termed hypersoft X-ray sources, predominantly radiate below 0.3 keV, near the boundary between X-rays and extreme ultraviolet light, making them difficult to detect because interstellar gas absorbs this radiation and standard surveys were not optimized for this spectral region. While their exact nature remains unconfirmed, candidates include white dwarfs, neutron stars, or black holes accreting from companion stars, with potential significant energy output in the form of invisible extreme-ultraviolet radiation. The discovery matters because such systems could represent a hidden source of ionizing radiation throughout galaxies and may include accreting white dwarfs that eventually evolve into Type Ia supernovae, which are critical cosmic distance markers. The finding demonstrates that re-examining existing data with new spectral questions can reveal previously missed populations without requiring new instruments.

telegram · science · Sep 14, 08:12

**「Background」** Most bright X-ray binaries are detected because they emit strongly above 0.3 keV, which is more easily observed. The hypersoft sources identified in this study emit primarily below this threshold, where radiation is heavily absorbed by intervening gas, explaining why they were missed by prior surveys. Type Ia supernovae are stellar explosions used as standard candles to measure cosmic distances, and identifying their progenitor systems is a key goal in astrophysics.

**「Impact」** Astrophysicists must revise galactic radiation budget models to include a newly identified source of ionizing extreme-ultraviolet flux from hypersoft X-ray binaries. The potential presence of undetected Type Ia supernova progenitors in these systems requires re-evaluation of supernova population models and distance calibration studies.
